## Acolyte Summoning
- enabled: True
- category: [9] Acolyte Acquisition
- starts off: False
- is custom text: False
- run on map init: False
```description
When rescued, an acolyte is immediately given a "Hold Position" order.  Doing so allows this trigger to respond to the order and keep track of the unit via the (Ordered unit) function.

Keeping track of the unit via a global variable is dangerous.  The trigger contains waits, and an additional simultaneous execution of the trigger could trash the value of a global variable while the trigger is still sleeping from the prior wait.
```
